```

    upd = aff + 2*a*(f-1)*p
    if k factor diag: upd[k] += a*a*(f*f-1) ? Wait at index j=k, s=k+j=2k, aff index j = s-k = j. Need add correction to the term? The diff formula `2*a*(f-1)*p[j]` for j=k gives 2a^2(f-1). Let's derive `_conv_full` efficiently. Use numpy rfft. For small N maybe direct? But we always N large. Implementation:
```
def _fft_conv(a,b):
    n=len(a); m=len(b); out_len=n+m-1
    L=1
    while L<out_len: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L); out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:out_len]
    return out
```
For autocorr same a, can use `fa= rfft(x,L); fa*fa; irfft`. Since `b=a`, use square not conjugate. This is faster. Implement `_conv_square(p)` specifically:
```
def _conv_square(p):
   n=len(p); out=2*n-1; L=_next_pow2(out)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   return np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L,:out)
```
The full convolution p*p with same sequence indeed product not conjugate. Floating errors small; clip negative? c may have tiny negative near zeros; max unaffected. For exact score, max(c) fine; but if all p positive, center positive.
```

```
Let's design MMD exact detailed pseudocode:
```
def _mmd_exact(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=50.0, beta_end=10000.0, memory=False):
    p=sanitize(p0)
    if None or time<=0: return p,score
    n=len(p)
    best=p.copy(); best_score=score(p); curr=p.copy(); curr_score=best_score
    alpha=0.2; stall=0; beta=beta_start
    deadline=...
    while time < deadline:
       c = conv_square(curr); d=n*c; maxd=c.max()*n
       if not finite: perturb
       # adaptive K
       if n<64: K=n
       else: K=min(2*n-1, max(16, (n//128)*4?)) maybe K=24? For n=4096, K=64.
       # get top K with partition
       if K < len(d):
           top_idx = np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:]
       else: top_idx=np.arange(len(d))
       # weights: uniform top K with softmax within top? global softmax with beta.
       beta = update_beta(d, top_idx, beta)
       w = softweights(d, beta, floor_w=1e-6)
       # h
       h = conv_weights(w, curr)
       h = where(finite, max(0))
       hmax=h.max()
       if hmax <= 1e-12:
           perturb
       else: h /= hmax
       # eta candidates
       etas = []
       for mult in (0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0):
           eta=alpha*mult
           if 1e-5 <= eta <= 50.0: etas.append(eta)
       if not etas: etas=[1e-4,1e-3,1e-2]
       best_eta=None; best_p=None; best_score_eta=curr_score
       for eta in etas:
           p_try = curr * np.exp(-eta*h)
           p_try = sanitize(p_try)
           if p_try is None: continue
           sc = exact_score(p_try)
           if sc < best_score_eta - 1e-12:
               best_score_eta=sc; best_eta=eta; best_p=p_try
       if best_p is not None:
           curr=best_p; curr_score=best_score_eta
           if curr_score < best_score - 1e-12:
               best=curr.copy(); best_score=curr_score; stall=0
               # update alpha based on best_eta rank
           else:
               # improve current but not best? maybe due curr worse than best. If curr_score <= best_score*1.05, keep; else reset
               if curr_score > best_score*1.1:
                   curr=best.copy(); curr_score=best_score
               stall=0? if improved current but not best, maybe small stall
       else:
           stall +=1
           alpha *=0.7
           if stall > 10:
              # perturbation
              std=0.03+0.002*min(stall,50)
              p_try=sanitize(curr*exp(rng.normal(0,std,n)))
              sc=score(p_try)
              if sc <= best_score*(1+0.01): curr=p_try; curr_score=sc
              else: curr=best.copy(); curr_score=best_score
              stall=0
           if stall > 60:
              curr=best; curr_score=best_score; alpha=0.2; stall=0
       # adaptive alpha increase if improvement and eta at high end
       if best_p is not None and best_eta is not None:
           if best_eta >= alpha*4: alpha=min(20, alpha*1.5)
           elif best_eta <= alpha*0.5: alpha=max(1e-5, alpha*0.8)
       # every 50 no best improve? perturb best? maybe
```
But this may be slow due exact score for each eta. We can optimize score via c = conv_square; no max(d) maybe. We can compute `exact_search(p)` as 2*n*np.max(c). If c has negative tiny, max. For speed, avoid normalization in score? Ensure p sanitized sum1.

Need `sanitize` in inner loops not too heavy (np.where, clip, sum). We can write a faster `_normalize_positive(vec)`:
```
vec = np.array(vec, dtype=float, copy=True)
vec[~np.isfinite(vec)] =0
vec[vec<0]=0
s=vec.sum()
if s<=0: return None
vec /= s
return vec
```
This modifies in place. When p_try = curr * exp(...), may underflow if eta*h large; if h max1, eta up to 20, exp(-20) ~2e-9 okay; underflow only if >700, not. Good.

Beta update: We need stable soft weights. Implementation:
```
def _make_weights(d, beta, n_len):
    if beta <=0: return full(1/len)
    shift=d.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(d-shift))
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0: e[...] =0; e[argmax]=1; s=1
    w=e/s
    # floor: w = (1-eps)*w + eps/len; renorm
    return w
```
If beta huge, e for non-top underflows to 0, top few with same d get equal. Good.
```

```
Let's write functions in detail.

1. `_next_pow2(x)`:
```
def _next_pow2(x):
    x=int(max(1,x))
    L=1
    while L<x: L <<=1
    return L
```

2. `_sanitize_vec(arr)`:
```
def _sanitize_vec(arr):
    p=np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim!=1 or p.size==0: return None
    p=np.array(p, dtype=float, copy=True)
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] =0.0
    p[p<0]=0.0
    # cap at 1000? if normalization changes but evaluator caps; internal can cap to avoid inf
    p[p>1000.0]=1000.0
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    p /= s
    return p
```
For speed, in inner line search we know vec finite nonnegative; can use `_fast_normalize(vec)`:
```
def _fast_normalize(vec):
   p=np.asarray(vec,dtype=float)
   p[~np.isfinite(p)] =0
   p[p<0]=0
   s=p.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return None
   p /= s
   return p
```
But modifying input? If vec is new array from multiplication, okay. If we pass best.p? We don't want mutate. Use copy where needed. We'll write `_normalize_copy(vec)`.

3. `_conv_square(p)` with L cached? Since same n per run, we can pass L to avoid pow2. Define `_fft_len(n)`.
```
def _conv_square(p, L=None):
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp, L)
   return c[:2*n-1]
```
For exact score, c may have tiny imaginary? irfft real.

4. `_score_from_c(p,c)`? `2.0*p.size*np.max(c)`.
`_exact_score(p)`:
```
p=sanitize; if None inf; c=conv; return 2*p.size*max(0,max(c))
```
For speed within MMD, pass p and L and function `_score_conv(c,n) = 2*n*max(c)`.

5. `_grad_h(w,p,L)`:
```
def _grad_h(w,p,L=None):
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=_next_pow2(2*n) # need at least w len? w len 2n-1, 2n ok; pow2 for 2n maybe same as conv L? For n=4096, _next_pow2(8191)=8192, _next_pow2(8192)=8192.
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L); fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conjugate(fp), L)
   return h[:n]
```
Could reuse rfft p if we computed? In MMD, we compute c via rfft p, then h needs rfft p again. Could combine: `_conv_square_and_h_weights`? We can compute p_fft once and reuse for c and h. Let's write a function `_conv_square_and_h(p, w, L)` that computes fp once, c, h.
```
def _conv_square_and_h(p, w, L):
   n=p.size; fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L); c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)[:2*n-1]
   if w is not None:
      fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L); h=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp),L)[:n]
      return c,h
   return c
```
This saves one rfft per iteration. But when evaluating candidate in line search, no h needed. We'll implement separate `_score_only` for candidates. For main iteration, use combined.

Actually for line search candidates, we need score only; can use `_conv_square(p_try,L)`. rfft p_try each candidate. Good.
```

def _top_weights(d, K, beta):
   L=len(d); top_idx=np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:] if K<L else arange
   w=zeros(L); vals=d[top_idx]
   shift=vals.max()
   exps=np.exp(beta*(vals-shift))
   w[top_idx]=exps
   # add small uniform
   w += 1e-6
   w /= w.sum()
   return w
```
This restricts gradient to top K plus tiny uniform. Good and faster than exp all. `beta` controls within top. K fixed. If K too small, maybe ignore. Choose K= max(16,min(2*n-1, 4*ceil(sqrt(2*n)))?) But we might pass a desired beta. Maybe use fixed beta high like 1000? Since top already restricts, beta can be moderate. def _resample(arr, N):
```
   a=sanitize
   n=a.size
   if n==N: return a.copy()
   x_old=(arange(n)+0.5)/n
   x_new=(arange(N)+0.5)/N
   out=interp(x_new,x_old,a)
   out[out<0]=0; out+=1e-15; return sanitize(out)
```

Let's draft code mentally.

```
import numpy as np
import time

def _next_pow2(x): ...

def _fast_normalize(vec):
    p=np.asarray(vec, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim !=1 or p.size==0: return None
    p=p.copy()
    bad=~np.isfinite(p)
    if np.any(bad): p[bad]=0.0
    if np.any(p<0): p[p<0]=0.0
    if np.any(p>1000): p[p>1000]=1000.0
    s=p.sum()
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    floor=1e-16/float(p.size)? maybe set later
    p/=s
    return p
```

`_make_support(N, m, rng, Kmax, span_end)`:
```
def _make_support(N, m, rng, Kmax, span_end=None):
   if span_end is None: span_end=N
   span_end=max(0,min(N,span_end))
   if span_end<=0 or m<=0: return np.array([],int)
   m=min(m,span_end)
   counts=np.zeros(2*N, dtype=float) # index up to 2N-2, size 2N ok
   support=[]
   order=rng.permutation(span_end)
   for x in order:
      if len(support)>=m: break
      x=int(x)
      # check double
      ds=2*x
      if ds < 2*N and counts[ds]+1 > Kmax: continue
      ok=True
      for y in support:
         s=x+y
         inc=2
         if s < 2*N and counts[s]+inc > Kmax:
             ok=False; break
      if not ok: continue
      for y in support:
         counts[x+y] += 2
      if ds < 2*N: counts[ds] += 1
      support.append(x)
   return np.array(support, dtype=int)
```
If Kmax small and m>feasible, may return fewer. Could retry with larger Kmax in caller. To speed, support list Python loop over support; for m=300, 45k per candidate; if scan many candidates maybe okay. If span_end large, first accepted early. For low Kmax and high m, many reject but still O. Limit attempts.
```

def _top_weights(d, K, beta_hint=None):
   L=len(d)
   if K>=L: idx=np.arange(L)
   else: idx=np.argpartition(d, -K)[-K:]

       w=np.zeros(L); w[idx]=1.0/K; # plus floor?

       beta = 3.0 / spread

   w += 1e-6 / L? That's tiny.

def _stage_search(N, budget, cands, rng, parent_arr, deadline):
    if budget <= 10 or N <= 0: return
    N=int(N)
    L=_next_pow2(2*N)
    starts=[]
    # parent
    if parent_arr is not None:
       sp=_resample(parent_arr,N); add if score finite
       sp=_resample(parent_arr[::-1],N)? Reversal before resample? Use np.asarray
    # top cand resample
    sorted_c=sorted(cands, key=_score_key)
    for item in sorted_c[:3]:
       sp=_resample(item[2],N); add
    # generate a few deterministic/random
    # To avoid too many, generate up to max_needed = 10
    if len(starts) < 6:
       modes = [0,1,2,3,4]
       for mode in modes:
           sp=_random_smooth_seed(N,rng,mode); add
    if len(starts) < 10:
       # base sparse
       settings = [(0.5,1.0,2),(0.45,1.5,4),(0.55,2.0,4),(0.6,3.0,8)]
       for r,mf,K in settings:
          sp=_base_sparse_seed(N,r,mf,K,rng,span=1.0); if not add
          break if enough
    # two-block quick
    sp=_two_block_seed(N,rng); add
    # evaluate
    scored=[]; seen? no need
    for sp in starts:
       sc=_exact_score_from_p(sp)
       if finite: scored.append((sc,sp))
    if not scored: return
    scored.sort(key=_score_key)
    # choose top unique maybe by score
    # allocate
    fracs=[0.5,0.25,0.25]
    best_score = scored[0][0]; best_arr=scored[0][1]
    it=0
    while it < len(scored) and (deadline - time.monotonic()) > 40:
       frac=fracs[it] if it<len(fracs) else 0.1
       talloc=frac*budget
       talloc=min(talloc, max(10, (deadline - time.monotonic()) - 20))
       if talloc < 10: break
       p,sc=_mmd_optimize(scored[it][1], rng, talloc, False)
       _add_candidate(cands,p,sc,20)
       if p is not None and sc<best_score:
          best_score=sc; best_arr=p
       it+=1
       # If we used top starts, after loop no break; maybe generate noisy variants if time
    # coordinate polish
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120:
       tpol=min(rem-30, 0.35*budget)
       p,sc=_coordinate_polish(best_arr, rng, tpol)
       _add_candidate(cands,p,sc,20)
       if p is not None and sc<best_score: best_score=sc
       rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
       if rem>80:
          p,sc=_mmd_optimize(best_arr, rng, min(rem-20, 0.3*budget), False)
          _add_candidate
    # add best
```

`_top_weights` implementation:
```
def _top_weights(d, K):
    L = d.size
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    K = int(min(L, max(1, K)))
    if K < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(d, L - K)[-K:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    vals = d[idx]
    vmax = float(np.max(vals))
    vmin = float(np.min(vals))
    spread = vmax - vmin
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if spread <= 1e-14:
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    else:
        beta = 3.0 / spread
        if beta > 1e5: beta = 1e5
        if beta < 1e-3: beta = 1e-3
        e = np.exp(beta * (vals - vmax))
        w[idx] = e
        w[idx] += 1e-12
    # small uniform floor
    w = (1.0 - 1e-5) * w + (1e-5 / float(L))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        w = np.zeros(L); w[idx] = 1.0/K; s=1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w
```
But if K large relative L and idx arange, adding floor and renorm okay.

`_conv_square_and_gradient` maybe:
```
def _conv_square_and_h(p, w, L):
    n=p.size
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)[:2*n-1]
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    h=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp),L)[:n]
    return c,h
```
This computes rfft w even if w same length. Good.

`_score_p(p,L=None)`: pass L if known.
```
def _score_p(p, L=None):
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
   c=_conv_square(p,L)
   mx=float(c.max())
   if not finite or mx<=0: return inf
   return float(2.0*n*mx)
```
But if p has exact zeros, c max could be 0 only if p all zero; no.

MMD uses `_score_p(p_try,L)` for candidates. It involves rfft p_try. Good.

MMD design with exact line search may be a bit slow evaluating 7 candidates. We can reduce by early stop if `time.monotonic()+candidate_est > deadline`? Not needed.

MMD code skeleton:
```
def _mmd_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, verbose=False):
    p = _fast_normalize(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 0:
       return p, _score_p(p) if p is not None else inf
    n=p.size
    L=_next_pow2(2*n)
    floor=1e-15/float(n)
    p=np.maximum(p, floor); p/=p.sum()
    c=_conv_square(p,L)
    best_score=float(2*n*c.max()); best=p.copy()
    curr=p.copy(); curr_score=best_score
    alpha=0.25
    K = max(8, min(2*n-1, max(8, (2*n-1)//128)))
    stall=0; no_best=0; it=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
    mults=(0.125,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       if it % 200 ==0 and verbose: print(...)
       c=_conv_square(curr,L)
       mx=float(c.max()); d=n*c
       if not finite mx: perturb
       w=_top_weights(d,K)
       h=_grad_h(w,curr,L)
       h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0)
       h=np.maximum(h,0.0)
       hmax=float(h.max())
       if hmax <= 1e-16:
          p_try= _perturb(curr,rng,0.05) ...; accept if not worse; stall++
          continue
       h /= hmax
       etas=[]
       for m in mults:
          eta=alpha*m
          if 1e-5 <= eta <= 50.0: etas.append(eta)
       if not etas: etas=[1e-4,1e-3]
       best_eta_score=curr_score; best_p=None; best_eta=None
       # maybe evaluate in random order? fixed.
       for eta in etas:
          if time.monotonic() >= deadline: break
          p_try=curr * np.exp(-eta*h)
          p_try=np.where(np.isfinite(p_try),p_try,0.0)
          p_try[p_try<0]=0.0
          s=float(p_try.sum())
          if s<=0 or not finite: continue
          p_try = p_try / s
          sc=_score_p(p_try,L)
          if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_eta_score - 1e-12:
             best_eta_score=sc; best_p=p_try; best_eta=eta
       if best_p is not None:
          curr=best_p; curr_score=best_eta_score
          # alpha adapt
          if best_eta is not None:
             if best_eta >= alpha*2.0:
                alpha=min(12.0, alpha*1.25)
             elif best_eta <= alpha*0.5:
                alpha=max(1e-5, alpha*0.85)
          stall=0
          if curr_score < best_score - 1e-12:
             best=curr.copy(); best_score=curr_score; no_best=0
          else:
             no_best += 1
             if curr_score > best_score*1.25:
                curr=best.copy(); curr_score=best_score
       else:
          stall +=1
          no_best +=1
          alpha=max(1e-5, alpha*0.7)
          if stall >= 5:
             p_try= _perturb(curr, rng, 0.05 + 0.005*min(stall,50))
             sc=_score_p(p_try,L)
             if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score*1.05:
                curr=p_try; curr_score=sc
             else:
                curr=best.copy(); curr_score=best_score
             stall=0
          if no_best > 300:
             # try bigger noise from best
             improved=False
             for trial_std in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6):
                p_try=_perturb(best,rng,trial_std)
                sc=_score_p(p_try,L)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                   best=p_try; best_score=sc; curr=p_try; curr_score=sc; improved=True; break
             if not improved:
                K = min(2*n-1, max(8, int(1.5*K)+4))
             no_best=0
             stall=0
             alpha=0.3
       it +=1
    # optional final coordinate? no.
    return best, best_score
```
`_perturb(p, rng, std)`:
```
def _perturb(p, rng, std):
   n=p.size
   noise=rng.normal(0.0, std, n)
   q = p * np.exp(noise)
   q = np.where(np.isfinite(q), q, 0.0)
   q = np.maximum(q, 0.0)
   s=q.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return p.copy()
   q /= s
   return q
```
Need not closure.

Potential issue: In line search, `curr * exp(-eta*h)` if h is all zero? handled. If eta large, p_try underflows to zeros for high h; sum may be tiny but positive. Fine.

`K` adaptation: K stored integer; if no_best reset increases. Need avoid K too large making uniform weights: if K > L/2 maybe fine. Keep max L.

Could lower K when improved significantly? Not needed.
```

def _two_block_seed(N,rng):
    p=np.ones(N,dtype=float)
    w=int(rng.integers(max(1,N//10), max(2,N-1)))
    ratio=float(rng.choice([0.5,0.8,1.2,1.6,2.2])) # rng.choice? `rng.choice` allowed but returns numpy scalar. To avoid, use list and rng.integers.
    p[:w]=ratio
    # maybe high at random location
    start=int(rng.integers(0, max(1,N-w+1)))
    p2=np.ones(N); p2[start:start+w]=ratio
    if rng.random()<0.5: return _fast_normalize(p2)
    return _fast_normalize(p)

def _score_p(p, L=None):
    if p is None: return inf
    n=p.size
    if L is None: L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    c=_conv_square(p,L)
    if c.size==0: return inf
    vals=c[np.isfinite(c)]
    if vals.size==0: return inf
    mx=float(vals.max())
    if mx <=0 or not finite: return inf
    return 2.0*n*mx

def _prune_candidates(cands, keep):

    cands.sort(key=_score_key)
    del cands[keep:]
    return cands
```

Now, `_coordinate_polish` details with recomputation due p normalization. Let's write robustly:
```
def _coordinate_polish(p0, rng, time_limit):
    p = _fast_normalize(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit<=0: return p, inf
    n=p.size; L=_next_pow2(2*n-1)
    floor=1e-15/float(n)
    p=np.maximum(p, floor); p=p/p.sum()
    c=_conv_square(p,L); score=2*n*c.max(); best=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
    K=max(8, min(2*n-1, (2*n-1)//128))
    factors=(0.4,0.6,0.75,0.9,1.1,1.25,1.5,1.8,2.5)
    h=None; iters=0; accepted=0; no_improve=0
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        if iters % 20 == 0:
            d=n*c
            w=_top_weights(d,K)
            h=_grad_h(w,p,L)
            h=np.where(np.isfinite(h),h,0.0); h=np.maximum(h,0.0); hmax=float(h.max())
            if hmax <= 1e-16: h=None
            else: h /= hmax
        if h is None:
            k=int(rng.integers(n))
        else:
            Kc=max(4, min(n, max(4, n//64)))
            top=np.argpartition(h, -Kc)[-Kc:]
            k=int(top[int(rng.integers(len(top)))])
        a=float(p[k])
        if a <= 0: iters+=1; continue
        # unaffected
        unaff=0.0
        if k>0:
            m1=float(c[:k].max())
            if m1>unaff: unaff=m1
        end=k+n
        if end < c.size:
            m2=float(c[end:].max())
            if m2>unaff: unaff=m2
        base_aff=c[k:k+n]
        best_f=1.0; best_score_cand=score; best_aff=None; best_S=1.0; best_S2=1.0
        # Optional: if no improvement often, try smaller factors? factors include.
        for f in factors:
            f=float(f)
            S=1.0 + a*(f-1.0)
            if S <= 1e-12: continue
            S2=S*S
            aff=base_aff + (2.0*a*(f-1.0))*p
            # correction at j=k in affected index k (since s=k+j, j=k)
            aff[k] += a*a*(f-1.0)*(f-1.0)
            local=float(aff.max())
            val=unaff if unaff>local else local
            cand_score = (val/S2) * (2.0*float(n))
            if cand_score < best_score_cand - 1e-12:
                best_score_cand=cand_score; best_f=f; best_aff=aff.copy(); best_S=S; best_S2=S2
        if best_f != 1.0:
            p[k] *= best_f
            p /= best_S
            # if floor? don't; but if small negative due floating? 
            p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            ssum=float(p.sum())
            if ssum <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ssum):
                p=best.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); score=best_score
            else:
                if abs(ssum-1.0) > 1e-8:
                    p /= ssum
                    # recalc c because normalization changed direct update
                    c=_conv_square(p,L); score=2*n*c.max()
                else:
                    c = c.copy()
                    c /= best_S2
                    c[k:k+n] = best_aff / best_S2
                    score=2.0*float(n)*float(c.max())
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score; no_improve=0
                else:
                    no_improve +=1
                accepted +=1
                if accepted % 100 == 0:
                    c=_conv_square(p,L); score=2*n*float(c.max())
                    if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                       best=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
        else:
            no_improve +=1
            if no_improve > 100:
                p_try=_perturb(p,rng,0.05)
                sc=_score_p(p_try,L)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                    p=p_try; c=_conv_square(p,L); score=2*n*c.max(); best=p.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_score=score
                no_improve=0
        iters +=1
        if iters % 100 == 0 and verbose? no
    return best, best_score
```
We need define `_score_p` can take L. In coordinate, use L.

Potential issue in coordinate direct update: We compute `unaff` from current c before scaling. If candidate S !=1, unaffected normalized = old_unaff / S^2. We compare `max(unaff, local)/S^2`, correct. But if accepting multiple candidates sequentially, c and p remain normalized. Good.
The direct formula for affected uses base_aff and p. For j=k double correction: We added `a*a*(f-1)^2`. Let's verify with p normalized, a old. Original contribution for s=2k: if i+j=2k, includes a^2 once. New f^2a^2. The vector formula `base_aff + 2a(f-1)p` at j=k adds 2a(f-1)a =2a^2(f-1). Need additional f^2a^2 - a^2 -2a^2(f-1) = a^2(f^2+1-2f)=a^2(f-1)^2. Yes.
For j≠k: original 2a p_j; new 2f a p_j; diff 2a(f-1)p_j. Good.
But what about coefficients where both i and j changed? Only one coordinate changed, so no.
What about p_j in formula should be old p_j before scaling? For unaffected coordinates, p_j old normalized. We haven't changed p yet. Good. For j=k, p old. Good.

If we recompute p normalized after update, p sum should exactly 1; if due floor/no floor, direct c update okay. We don't enforce floor. If p[k] underflows to 0, future direct updates for k with a=0 skip; okay.

In coordinate, after accepting and updating c, `score=2*n*c.max()`. Could c have tiny negative due previous FFT; direct update may keep. `max` okay. If c gets negative everywhere? no.

Now, `_stage_search` uses final best from cands; but after MMD, cands may have arrays from smaller N. For coordinate at N, we should resample best to N before polish. Use `best_item=min(cands,key=_score_key); pN=_resample(best_item[2], N); polish pN`. Good.

At the end of stage, we might have best at smaller N but cands stores. In propose final, choose min internal score; likely larger N. Good.

Potential problem: Linear resampling of a sparse high-weight sequence to larger N reduces max weights, may change score; but MMD can adjust. If score after resample is worse, the optimized result may not improve. However, for a good shape, resampling should not increase too much. We can also use a "zero-preserving" upsample: For factor 2, interleave p with p? Actually to approximate same continuous shape, linear is good. If parent has point masses, linear spreads; but optimum at finer can concentrate. Optimizer can increase some coordinates.

Maybe final stage from previous parent length 3072 to 8192 by interpolation may take MMD to reconcentrate; time limited. ```
```
def _sparse_plus_base_seed(base_arr, N, r, mfac, Kmax, span, rng):
    r=np.clip(r,0.05,0.95); s=1-r
    if base_arr is None:
        base=np.full(N, 1.0/N)
    else:
        base=_resample(base_arr, N)
        if base is None: base=np.full(N,1.0/N)
        base=_fast_normalize(base)
    support=_make_support(N, int(max(2,mfac*np.sqrt(N))), rng, Kmax, int(span*N))
    if len(support) < max(2, int(0.5*mfac*np.sqrt(N))): return None
    p = r*base
    w=np.full(len(support), 1.0/len(support))
    p[support] += s*w
    return _fast_normalize(p)
```

`_two_block_seed`:
```
def _two_block_seed(N, rng):
    p = np.ones(N, dtype=float)
    w = int(rng.integers(max(1, N//20), max(2, N-1)))
    w=min(w,N-1)
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, N-w+1)))
    ratio = [0.4,0.7,1.3,2.0,3.0][int(rng.integers(5))]
    p[start:start+w] = ratio
    # also maybe prefix
    if rng.random() < 0.5:
        p2 = np.ones(N)
        p2[:w] = ratio
        return _fast_normalize(p2)
    return _fast_normalize(p)
```
No nested.
```

```
`_fast_normalize`:
```
def _fast_normalize(arr):
    p = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = p.copy()
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    p[p < 0.0] = 0.0
    p[p > 1000.0] = 1000.0
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    p /= s
    return p
```
If p has huge >1000 and sum, after cap sum may be <1? We then normalize; this mimics evaluator cap but internal okay. If cap makes sum zero (all inf?) handled.
```

```
`_grad_h`:
```
def _grad_h(w,p,L=None):
   n=p.size
   if L is None: L=_next_pow2(2*n)
   fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
   h=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp), L)
   return h[:n]

```python
"""
Multi-start search for non-negative sequences minimizing

    score = 2 * n * max(conv(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

The implementation works on the probability simplex (sum = 1), where the
objective is 2 * n * max(conv(a, a)).  It combines:

- length refinement (small -> medium -> large),
- varied initializations: previous best if present, parent/resampled
  candidates, low-degree smooth random shapes, two-block shapes, and
  "base + sparse Sidon-like support" seeds,
- exact line-search multiplicative descent using active-set weights,
- coordinate-scaling local search with maintained convolution values.

All helper functions are top-level and avoid closures/lambdas.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _fast_normalize(arr):
    p = np.asarray(arr, dtype=float)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return None
    p = p.copy()
    p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
    p[p < 0.0] = 0.0
    p[p > 1000.0] = 1000.0
    s = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    p /= s
    return p


def _score_norm(p, L=None):
    if p is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    if L is None:
        L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c_f = c[np.isfinite(c)]
    if c_f.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    mx = float(c_f.max())
    if not np.isfinite(mx):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, mx))


def _exact_score_from_p(p):
    a = _fast_normalize(p)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf")
    n = int(a.size)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    return _score_norm(a, L)


def _score_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _prune_candidates(cands, keep=12):
    if len(cands) <= keep:
        return cands
    cands.sort(key=_score_key)
    del cands[keep:]
    return cands


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, score, keep=12):
    a = _fast_normalize(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score = _score_norm(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return
    cands.append((float(score), int(a.size), a.copy()))
    _prune_candidates(cands, keep)


def _add_start(starts, arr, limit=20):
    if arr is None:
        return
    a = _fast_normalize(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if len(starts) < limit:
        starts.append(a)


def _resample(arr, m):
    a = _fast_normalize(arr)
    if a is None or m <= 0:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.full(m, 1.0 / float(m), dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    out[out < 0.0] = 0.0
    out += 1e-15
    return _fast_normalize(out)


def _perturb(p, rng, std):
    a = _fast_normalize(p)
    if a is None:
        return None
    n = a.size
    q = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, float(std), n))
    q[~np.isfinite(q)] = 0.0
    q[q < 0.0] = 0.0
    q = np.maximum(q, 1e-15 / float(n))
    s = float(q.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return a.copy()
    q /= s
    return q


def _top_weights(d, K):
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    L = int(d.size)
    if L <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    K = int(min(L, max(1, K)))
    if K < L:
        idx = np.argpartition(d, L - K)[-K:]
    else:
        idx = np.arange(L)
    vals = d[idx]
    vmax = float(np.max(vals))
    vmin = float(np.min(vals))
    spread = vmax - vmin
    w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
    if spread <= 1e-14:
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
    else:
        beta = 3.0 / spread
        beta = min(1.0e5, max(1.0e-3, beta))
        e = np.exp(beta * (vals - vmax))
        w[idx] = e
        w[idx] += 1e-12
    w = (1.0 - 1e-5) * w + (1e-5 / float(L))
    s = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=float)
        w[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)
        s = 1.0
    else:
        w /= s
    return w


def _grad_h(w, p, L=None):
    n = int(p.size)
    if L is None:
        L = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    h = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return h[:n]


def _make_support(N, m, rng, Kmax, span_end=None):
    N = int(N)
    m = int(max(2, m))
    if N <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=int)
    if span_end is None:
        span_end = N
    span_end = int(max(2, min(N, span_end)))
    m = min(m, span_end)
    counts = np.zeros(2 * N, dtype=float)
    support = []
    order = rng.permutation(span_end)
    for x_raw in order:
        if len(support) >= m:
            break
        x = int(x_raw)
        ds = 2 * x
        if ds < 2 * N and counts[ds] + 1.0 > float(Kmax):
            continue
        ok = True
        for y in support:
            ssum = x + y
            if ssum < 2 * N and counts[ssum] + 2.0 > float(Kmax):
                ok = False
                break
        if not ok:
            continue
        for y in support:
            counts[x + y] += 2.0
        if ds < 2 * N:
            counts[ds] += 1.0
        support.append(x)
    return np.asarray(support, dtype=int)


def _sparse_plus_base_seed(base_arr, N, r, mfac, Kmax, span, rng):
    N = int(N)
    r = float(min(0.95, max(0.05, r)))
    s = 1.0 - r
    if base_arr is None:
        base = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=float)
    else:
        base = _resample(base_arr, N)
        if base is None:
            base = np.full(N, 1.0 / float(N), dtype=float)
    span_end = int(max(2, min(N, max(2, int(round(span * N))))))
    m = int(max(2, mfac * float(np.sqrt(N))))
    support = _make_support(N, m, rng, Kmax, span_end)
    if support.size < max(2, int(0.5 * m)):
        return None
    p = r * base
    w = np.full(support.size, 1.0 / float(support.size), dtype=float)
    p[support] += s * w
    return _fast_normalize(p)


def _random_smooth_seed(N, rng, mode=0):
    N = int(N)
    t = (np.arange(N, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(N)
    if mode == 0:
        p = np.ones(N, dtype=float)
        for k in range(1, 12):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / (k + 1)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            p += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        p = np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(p, -50.0, 50.0)))
    elif mode == 1:
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 3.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.15, 3.0))
        tt = np.clip(t, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
        p = np.power(tt, a) * np.power(1.0 - tt, b)
    elif mode == 2:
        K = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        p = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
        for i in range(K):
            c = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0))
            sd = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.35))
            wt = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0))
            p += wt * np.exp(-((t - c) / sd) ** 2)
    elif mode == 3:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 9))
        knots = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.interp(t, knots, heights)
    elif mode == 4:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 16))
        edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, max(1, K - 1)))
        blocks = [0.0]
        blocks.extend(list(edges))
        blocks.append(1.0)
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.zeros(N, dtype=float)
        for i in range(K):
            a = int(round(blocks[i] * N))
            b = int(round(blocks[i + 1] * N))
            if b <= a:
                b = min(N, a + 1)
            p[a:b] = float(heights[i])
    else:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.25, 0.75))
        k = float(rng.uniform(6.0, 25.0))
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(k * (t - c), -50.0, 50.0)))
        p *= np.exp(-((t - c) / 0.22) ** 2)
    return _fast_normalize(p)


def _two_block_seed(N, rng):
    N = int(N)
    p = np.ones(N, dtype=float)
    w = int(rng.integers(max(1, N // 20), max(2, N - 1)))
    w = min(max(1, w), max(1, N - 1))
    start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, N - w + 1)))
    ratios = (0.35, 0.65, 1.25, 2.0, 3.2)
    ratio = float(ratios[int(rng.integers(len(ratios)))])
    p[start:start + w] = ratio
    if float(rng.random()) < 0.5:
        q = np.ones(N, dtype=float)
        q[:w] = ratio
        return _fast_normalize(q)
    return _fast_normalize(p)


def _mmd_optimize(p0, rng, time_limit, verbose=False):
    p = _fast_normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    n = int(p.size)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    floor = 1e-15 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p /= p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_norm(p, L)
    curr = p.copy()
    curr_score = best_score

    alpha = 0.25
    K = max(8, min(2 * n - 1, max(8, (2 * n - 1) // 128)))
    stall = 0
    no_best = 0
    it = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    multis = (0.125, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if verbose and it % 250 == 0:
            print(f"    mmd n={n} iter={it} best={best_score:.6f} curr={curr_score:.6f}")

        c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(curr, L) * np.fft.rfft(curr, L), L)[:2 * n - 1]
        c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
        mx = float(c.max()) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            p_try = _perturb(curr, rng, 0.05)
            if p_try is not None:
                sc = _score_norm(p_try, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.1:
                    curr = p_try
                    curr_score = sc
                else:
                    curr = best.copy()
                    curr_score = best_score
            stall += 1
            continue

        d = n * c
        w = _top_weights(d, K)
        h = _grad_h(w, curr, L)
        h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        hmax = float(h.max())
        if hmax <= 1e-16:
            p_try = _perturb(curr, rng, 0.06)
            if p_try is not None:
                sc = _score_norm(p_try, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.1:
                    curr = p_try
                    curr_score = sc
                else:
                    curr = best.copy()
                    curr_score = best_score
            stall += 1
            continue

        h = h / hmax
        etas = []
        for mlt in multis:
            eta = float(alpha) * float(mlt)
            if 1e-6 <= eta <= 50.0:
                etas.append(eta)
        if not etas:
            etas = [1e-4, 1e-3, 1e-2]

        best_eta_score = curr_score
        best_eta_p = None
        best_eta = None
        for eta in etas:
            p_try = curr * np.exp(-eta * h)
            p_try[~np.isfinite(p_try)] = 0.0
            p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
            s = float(p_try.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            p_try /= s
            sc = _score_norm(p_try, L)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_eta_score - 1e-12:
                best_eta_score = sc
                best_eta_p = p_try
                best_eta = float(eta)

        if best_eta_p is not None:
            curr = best_eta_p
            curr_score = float(best_eta_score)
            if best_eta is not None:
                if best_eta >= alpha * 2.0:
                    alpha = min(12.0, alpha * 1.25)
                elif best_eta <= alpha * 0.5:
                    alpha = max(1e-6, alpha * 0.85)
            stall = 0
            if curr_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best = curr.copy()
                best_score = curr_score
                no_best = 0
            else:
                no_best += 1
                if curr_score > best_score * 1.25:
                    curr = best.copy()
                    curr_score = best_score
        else:
            stall += 1
            no_best += 1
            alpha = max(1e-6, alpha * 0.7)
            if stall >= 5:
                std = 0.05 + 0.004 * float(min(stall, 60))
                p_try = _perturb(curr, rng, std)
                sc = _score_norm(p_try if p_try is not None else curr, L)
                if p_try is not None and np.isfinite(sc) and sc <= best_score * 1.05:
                    curr = p_try
                    curr_score = sc
                else:
                    curr = best.copy()
                    curr_score = best_score
                stall = 0
            if no_best > 300:
                improved = False
                for std in (0.08, 0.15, 0.25, 0.45):
                    p_try = _perturb(best, rng, std)
                    if p_try is None:
                        continue
                    sc = _score_norm(p_try, L)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-13:
                        best = p_try
                        best_score = sc
                        curr = p_try
                        curr_score = sc
                        improved = True
                        break
                if not improved:
                    K = min(2 * n - 1, max(8, int(1.5 * K) + 4))
                no_best = 0
                stall = 0
                alpha = 0.35

        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def _coordinate_polish(p0, rng, time_limit):
    p = _fast_normalize(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit <= 0:
        return p, _score_norm(p)

    n = int(p.size)
    L = _next_pow2(2 * n)
    floor = 1e-15 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p /= p.sum()

    c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p, L) * np.fft.rfft(p, L), L)[:2 * n - 1]
    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, c.max()))
    best = p.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_score = score

    K = max(8, min(2 * n - 1, max(8, (2 * n - 1) // 128)))
    factors = (0.4, 0.6, 0.75, 0.9, 1.1, 1.25, 1.5, 1.8, 2.5)
    h = None
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    accepted = 0
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        if it % 25 == 0:
            d = n * c
            w = _top_weights(d, K)
            h = _grad_h(w, p, L)
            h[~np.isfinite(h)] = 0.0
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            hmax = float(h.max())
            if hmax <= 1e-16:
                h = None
            else:
                h = h / hmax

        if h is None:
            k = int(rng.integers(n))
        else:
            Kc = max(4, min(n, max(4, n // 64)))
            if Kc >= n:
                k = int(rng.integers(n))
            else:
                top = np.argpartition(h, n - Kc)[-Kc:]
                k = int(top[int(rng.integers(len(top)))])

        a = float(p[k])
        if a <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(a):
            it += 1
            continue

        unaff = 0.0
        if k > 0:
            u1 = float(np.max(c[:k])) if c[:k].size else 0.0
            if u1 > unaff:
                unaff = u1
        end = k + n
        if end < c.size:
            u2 = float(np.max(c[end:])) if c[end:].size else 0.0
            if u2 > unaff:
                unaff = u2

        base_aff = c[k:k + n]
        best_f = 1.0
        best_cand_score = score
        best_aff_new = None
        best_S = 1.0
        best_S2 = 1.0

        for f in factors:
            f = float(f)
            S = 1.0 + a * (f - 1.0)
            if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
                continue
            S2 = S * S
            aff = base_aff + (2.0 * a * (f - 1.0)) * p
            aff[k] += a * a * (f - 1.0) * (f - 1.0)
            local = float(aff.max())
            val = unaff if unaff > local else local
            cand_score = (val / S2) * (2.0 * float(n))
            if cand_score < best_cand_score - 1e-12:
                best_cand_score = cand_score
                best_f = f
                best_aff_new = aff.copy()
                best_S = S
                best_S2 = S2

        if best_f != 1.0:
            p[k] *= best_f
            p /= best_S
            p[~np.isfinite(p)] = 0.0
            p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
            ssum = float(p.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(ssum) or ssum <= 0.0:
                p = best.copy()
                c = best_c.copy()
                score = best_score
            else:
                if abs(ssum - 1.0) > 1e-8:
                    p /= ssum
                    c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p, L) * np.fft.rfft(p, L), L)[:2 * n - 1]
                    c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
                    score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, c.max()))
                else:
                    c = c.copy()
                    c /= best_S2
                    c[k:k + n] = best_aff_new / best_S2
                    score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, c.max()))

                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
                    best_score = score
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

            accepted += 1
            if accepted % 100 == 0:
                c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p, L) * np.fft.rfft(p, L), L)[:2 * n - 1]
                c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
                score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, c.max()))
                if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_c = c.copy()
                    best_score = score
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 120:
                q = _perturb(p, rng, 0.06)
                if q is not None:
                    sc = _score_norm(q, L)
                    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                        p = q
                        c = np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(p, L) * np.fft.rfft(p, L), L)[:2 * n - 1]
                        c[~np.isfinite(c)] = 0.0
                        score = float(2.0 * n * max(0.0, c.max()))
                        best = p.copy()
                        best_c = c.copy()
                        best_score = score
                no_improve = 0

        it += 1

    return best, best_score


def _stage_search(N, budget, cands, rng, parent_arr, deadline):
    N = int(N)
    budget = float(budget)
    if N <= 0 or budget <= 5.0:
        return

    start_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(5.0, budget))
    starts = []

    if parent_arr is not None:
        _add_start(starts, _resample(parent_arr, N))
        _add_start(starts, _resample(np.asarray(parent_arr, dtype=float)[::-1], N))

    if cands:
        sorted_c = sorted(cands, key=_score_key)
        for item in sorted_c[:3]:
            _add_start(starts, _resample(item[2], N))

    if len(starts) < 6:
        for mode in range(5):
            _add_start(starts, _random_smooth_seed(N, rng, mode))

    settings = [
        (0.45, 1.0, 2, 0.50),
        (0.50, 1.2, 4, 0.50),
        (0.55, 1.5, 4, 0.50),
        (0.55, 2.0, 8, 0.75),
        (0.40, 1.0, 2, 0.33),
    ]
    if N >= 4096:
        settings = settings[:3]

    bases = [None]
    if parent_arr is not None:
        bases.append(parent_arr)

    for base in bases:
        for rr, mf, kmax, span in settings:
            if len(starts) >= 18:
                break
            sp = _sparse_plus_base_seed(base, N, rr, mf, kmax, span, rng)
            _add_start(starts, sp, 20)

    for _ in range(2):
        _add_start(starts, _two_block_seed(N, rng), 20)

    scored = []
    for sp in starts:
        sc = _exact_score_from_p(sp)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((sc, sp))
    if not scored:
        return
    scored.sort(key=_score_key)

    fracs = (0.45, 0.30, 0.25)
    idx = 0
    while idx < len(scored) and time.monotonic() < start_deadline - 60.0:
        rem = start_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.15
        talloc = max(12.0, budget * frac)
        talloc = min(talloc, max(12.0, rem - 15.0))
        p, sc = _mmd_optimize(scored[idx][1], rng, talloc, verbose=(idx == 0))
        _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 18)
        idx += 1

    extra = 0
    while extra < 2 and time.monotonic() < start_deadline - 80.0 and cands:
        rem = start_deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 35.0:
            break
        best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
        p0 = _resample(best_item[2], N)
        p0 = _perturb(p0, rng, 0.06)
        if p0 is None:
            break
        talloc = min(120.0, rem - 25.0)
        if talloc < 20.0:
            break
        p, sc = _mmd_optimize(p0, rng, talloc, verbose=False)
        _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 18)
        extra += 1

    rem = start_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if cands and N >= 1024 and rem > 90.0:
        best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
        p_pol = _resample(best_item[2], N)
        if p_pol is not None:
            tpol = min(0.28 * budget, rem - 30.0)
            if tpol > 25.0:
                p, sc = _coordinate_polish(p_pol, rng, tpol)
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 18)

    rem = start_deadline - time.monotonic()
    if cands and rem > 60.0:
        best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
        p_mmd = _resample(best_item[2], N)
        if p_mmd is not None:
            tmmd = min(0.18 * budget, rem - 20.0)
            if tmmd > 20.0:
                p, sc = _mmd_optimize(p_mmd, rng, tmmd, verbose=False)
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 18)

    if cands:
        best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
        print(f"stage N={N} budget={budget:.0f}s global_best={best_item[0]:.6f}")


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400.0, **kwargs):
    """
    Returns a non-negative list of floats intended to minimize
    evaluate_sequence(sequence).
    """
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        raw_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        raw_budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(raw_budget) or raw_budget <= 0.0:
        raw_budget = 2400.0

    if raw_budget < 120.0:
        deadline = start_time + max(1.0, 0.9 * raw_budget)
    else:
        deadline = start_time + max(60.0, raw_budget - 30.0)

    try:
        iseed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        iseed = 12345
    rng = np.random.default_rng((iseed % 1000000007) + 7)

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        parent_raw = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        parent_arr = _fast_normalize(parent_raw)

    cands = []

    if parent_arr is not None:
        _add_candidate(cands, parent_arr, _exact_score_from_p(parent_arr), 12)

    try:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 300.0:
            tA = min(160.0, rem * 0.16)
            if tA > 20.0:
                _stage_search(512, tA, cands, rng, parent_arr, deadline)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 800.0:
            tB = min(700.0, rem * 0.30)
            if tB > 40.0:
                _stage_search(2048, tB, cands, rng, parent_arr, deadline)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 650.0:
            tC = min(1450.0, rem * 0.58)
            if tC > 60.0:
                _stage_search(4096, tC, cands, rng, parent_arr, deadline)

        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 320.0:
            tD = min(900.0, rem - 50.0)
            if tD > 40.0:
                _stage_search(8192, tD, cands, rng, parent_arr, deadline)

        # Final absolute polish.
        if cands:
            best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem > 90.0:
                tpol = min(180.0, rem - 30.0)
                p, sc = _coordinate_polish(best_item[2], rng, tpol)
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)

            if cands:
                best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 80.0:
                    tmmd = min(140.0, rem - 25.0)
                    p, sc = _mmd_optimize(best_item[2], rng, tmmd, verbose=False)
                    _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)

            # If no large-length search could be afforded, try one final
            # larger refinement.
            if cands:
                best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if best_item[2].size < 4096 and rem > 260.0:
                    big_n = min(8192, max(4096, 2 * int(best_item[2].size)))
                    p_big = _resample(best_item[2], big_n)
                    if p_big is not None:
                        p, sc = _mmd_optimize(p_big, rng, min(rem - 60.0, 500.0), verbose=False)
                        _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)
                        if cands:
                            best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
                            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                            if rem > 60.0:
                                p2, sc2 = _coordinate_polish(best_item[2], rng, min(rem - 20.0, 120.0))
                                _add_candidate(cands, p2, sc2, 16)
    except Exception:
        pass

    if not cands or all(x[2] is None for x in cands):
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=float)
        out /= float(out.size)
        return [float(x) for x in out]

    best_item = min(cands, key=_score_key)
    out = _fast_normalize(best_item[2])
    if out is None:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=float)
        out /= float(out.size)

    out = np.asarray(out, dtype=float)
    out[~np.isfinite(out)] = 0.0
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(1024, dtype=float)
        out /= float(out.size)
    else:
        out /= s

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
